## [Third Age](/wiki/Third_Age) {#Third_Age}

<table class="wikitable">
<!-- -->
<tbody><tr>
<th><b><a href="/wiki/Third_Age" title="Third Age">T.A.</a></b></th>
<th><b>Event</b>
</th></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1" title="Third Age 1">1</a></td>
<td>The Third Age begins.<sup id="cite_ref-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-1">[1]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_2" title="Third Age 2">2</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Isildur" title="Isildur">Isildur</a> plants a <a href="/wiki/White_Tree_of_Gondor#Second_White_Tree" title="White Tree of Gondor">Sapling</a> of the <a href="/wiki/White_Tree_of_Gondor#First_White_Tree" title="White Tree of Gondor">White Tree</a> in <a href="/wiki/Minas_Tirith" title="Minas Tirith">Minas Anor</a>.<br/><b>Death of <a href="/wiki/Isildur" title="Isildur">Isildur</a> and his sons <a href="/wiki/Elendur_(son_of_Isildur)" title="Elendur (son of Isildur)">Elendur</a>, <a href="/wiki/Aratan" title="Aratan">Aratan</a> and <a href="/wiki/Ciryon" title="Ciryon">Ciryon</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Disaster_of_the_Gladden_Fields" title="Disaster of the Gladden Fields">Disaster of the Gladden Fields</a>. <a href="/wiki/The_One_Ring" title="The One Ring">The One Ring</a> is lost.</b> <a href="/wiki/Meneldil" title="Meneldil">Meneldil</a>, son of <a href="/wiki/An%C3%A1rion" title="Anárion">Anárion</a>, becomes the third <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a> and the first sole ruler of the kingdom.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_3" title="Third Age 3">3</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Ohtar" title="Ohtar">Ohtar</a> brings the <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Shards_of_Narsil" title="Shards of Narsil">Shards of Narsil</a> to <a href="/wiki/Rivendell" title="Rivendell">Imladris</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_10" title="Third Age 10">10</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Valandil_(King_of_Arnor)" title="Valandil (King of Arnor)">Valandil</a> becomes the third <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arnor" title="Kings of Arnor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_48" title="Third Age 48">48</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rendil_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Eärendil (King of Gondor)">Eärendil</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_87" title="Third Age 87">87</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Eldacar_(King_of_Arnor)" title="Eldacar (King of Arnor)">Eldacar</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_109" title="Third Age 109">109</a></td>
<td><b><a href="/wiki/Elrond" title="Elrond">Elrond</a> and <a href="/wiki/Celebr%C3%ADan" title="Celebrían">Celebrían</a> wed.</b><sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_130" title="Third Age 130">130</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Elladan" title="Elladan">Elladan</a> and <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Elrohir" title="Elrohir">Elrohir</a>, twin sons of <a href="/wiki/Elrond" title="Elrond">Elrond</a> and <a href="/wiki/Celebr%C3%ADan" title="Celebrían">Celebrían</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_136" title="Third Age 136">136</a></td>

<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_158" title="Third Age 158">158</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Meneldil" title="Meneldil">Meneldil</a>. <a href="/wiki/Cemendur_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Cemendur (King of Gondor)">Cemendur</a> becomes the fourth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>

</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_222" title="Third Age 222">222</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Ostoher" title="Ostoher">Ostoher</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>

<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_238" title="Third Age 238">238</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Cemendur_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Cemendur (King of Gondor)">Cemendur</a>. <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rendil_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Eärendil (King of Gondor)">Eärendil</a> becomes the fifth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>

</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_249" title="Third Age 249">249</a></td>

</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_324" title="Third Age 324">324</a></td>

</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_515" title="Third Age 515">515</a></td>

<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_736" title="Third Age 736">736</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rnil_I" title="Eärnil I">Eärnil I</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_748" title="Third Age 748">748</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Atanatar_I" title="Atanatar I">Atanatar I</a> <a href="/wiki/Siriondil_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Siriondil (King of Gondor)">Siriondil</a> becomes the eleventh <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_777" title="Third Age 777">777</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arnor" title="Kings of Arnor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Elendur_(King_of_Arnor)" title="Elendur (King of Arnor)">Elendur</a>. <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rendur_(King_of_Arnor)" title="Eärendur (King of Arnor)">Eärendur</a> becomes the tenth and last <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arnor" title="Kings of Arnor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_811" title="Third Age 811">811</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Beleg_(King_of_Arthedain)" title="Beleg (King of Arthedain)">Beleg</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_820" title="Third Age 820">820</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Ciryandil" title="Ciryandil">Ciryandil</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_830" title="Third Age 830">830</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Siriondil_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Siriondil (King of Gondor)">Siriondil</a>. <a href="/wiki/Tarannon_Falastur" title="Tarannon Falastur">Tarannon Falastur</a> becomes the twelfth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>, beginning the line of <a href="/wiki/Ship-kings" title="Ship-kings">Ship-kings</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-South_5-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_861" title="Third Age 861">861</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arnor" title="Kings of Arnor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rendur_(King_of_Arnor)" title="Eärendur (King of Arnor)">Eärendur (King of Arnor)</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>. <b><a href="/wiki/Arnor#Decline_and_Breakup" title="Arnor">Division of Arnor</a> into <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>, <a href="/wiki/Rhudaur" title="Rhudaur">Rhudaur</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Cardolan" title="Cardolan">Cardolan</a>.</b><sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-7">[7]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rendur_(King_of_Arnor)" title="Eärendur (King of Arnor)">Eärendur</a>&#39;s eldest son, <a href="/wiki/Amlaith" title="Amlaith">Amlaith</a> of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Fornost" title="Fornost">Fornost</a>, becomes the first <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_895" title="Third Age 895">895</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Mallor" title="Mallor">Mallor</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_899" title="Third Age 899">899</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Hyarmendacil_I" title="Hyarmendacil I">Ciryaher</a> (later <i>Hyarmedacil I</i>) of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_913" title="Third Age 913">913</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Tarannon_Falastur" title="Tarannon Falastur">Tarannon Falastur</a>. <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rnil_I" title="Eärnil I">Eärnil I</a> becomes the thirteenth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a> and second <a href="/wiki/Ship-kings" title="Ship-kings">Ship-king</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_933" title="Third Age 933">933</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Umbar" title="Umbar">Umbar</a> seized by <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rnil_I" title="Eärnil I">Eärnil I</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_936" title="Third Age 936">936</a></td>
<td>Drowning of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rnil_I" title="Eärnil I">Eärnil I</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Ciryandil" title="Ciryandil">Ciryandil</a> becomes the fourteenth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a> and third <a href="/wiki/Ship-kings" title="Ship-kings">Ship-king</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_946" title="Third Age 946">946</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Amlaith" title="Amlaith">Amlaith</a>. <a href="/wiki/Beleg_(King_of_Arthedain)" title="Beleg (King of Arthedain)">Beleg</a> becomes the second <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_977" title="Third Age 977">977</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Atanatar_II" title="Atanatar II">Atanatar II</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_979" title="Third Age 979">979</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Celepharn" title="Celepharn">Celepharn</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1000" title="Third Age 1000">1000</a></td>
<td><b>About this time the <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Istari" title="Istari">Istari</a> first appear in <a href="/wiki/Middle-earth" title="Middle-earth">Middle-earth</a>.</b>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1015" title="Third Age 1015">1015</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Ciryandil" title="Ciryandil">Ciryandil</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Hyarmendacil_I" title="Hyarmendacil I">Ciryaher</a> (later <i>Hyarmendacil I</i>) becomes the fifteenth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a> and last of its <a href="/wiki/Ship-kings" title="Ship-kings">Ship-kings</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1029" title="Third Age 1029">1029</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Beleg_(King_of_Arthedain)" title="Beleg (King of Arthedain)">Beleg</a>. <a href="/wiki/Mallor" title="Mallor">Mallor</a> becomes the third <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1049" title="Third Age 1049">1049</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Narmacil_I" title="Narmacil I">Narmacil I</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1050" title="Third Age 1050">1050</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Hyarmendacil_I" title="Hyarmendacil I">Ciryaher</a> conquers <a href="/wiki/Harad" title="Harad">Harad</a> and takes the name <i>Hyarmendacil</i>, &#34;South-victor&#34;, in token of this. <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a> reaches the height of its power.<br/>About this time a shadow falls on <a href="/wiki/Mirkwood" title="Mirkwood">Greenwood</a>, and men begin to call it <a href="/wiki/Mirkwood" title="Mirkwood">Mirkwood</a>.<br/>The <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Periannath" title="Periannath">Periannath</a> are first mentioned in records with the coming of the <a href="/wiki/Harfoots" title="Harfoots">Harfoots</a> to <a href="/wiki/Eriador" title="Eriador">Eriador</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1058" title="Third Age 1058">1058</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Calmacil_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Calmacil (King of Gondor)">Calmacil</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1062" title="Third Age 1062">1062</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Celebrindor" title="Celebrindor">Celebrindor</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1100" title="Third Age 1100">1100</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/The_Wise" title="The Wise">The Wise</a> (the <a href="/wiki/Wizards" title="Wizards">Istari</a> and chief <a href="/wiki/Eldar" title="Eldar">Eldar</a>) discover that an evil power has made a stronghold at <a href="/wiki/Dol_Guldur" title="Dol Guldur">Dol Guldur</a>. It is thought to be one of the <a href="/wiki/Nazg%C3%BBl" title="Nazgûl">Nazgûl</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-16" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1110" title="Third Age 1110">1110</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Mallor" title="Mallor">Mallor</a>. <a href="/wiki/Celepharn" title="Celepharn">Celepharn</a> becomes the fourth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1126" title="Third Age 1126">1126</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/R%C3%B3mendacil_II" title="Rómendacil II">Minalcar</a> (later <i>Rómendacil II</i>) of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1144" title="Third Age 1144">1144</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Malvegil" title="Malvegil">Malvegil</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1149" title="Third Age 1149">1149</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Hyarmendacil_I" title="Hyarmendacil I">Hyarmendacil I</a>. <a href="/wiki/Atanatar_II" title="Atanatar II">Atanatar II Alcarin</a> becomes the sixteenth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-South_5-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1150" title="Third Age 1150">1150</a></td>
<td>About this time, the <a href="/wiki/Fallohides" title="Fallohides">Fallohides</a> enter <a href="/wiki/Eriador" title="Eriador">Eriador</a>. The <a href="/wiki/Stoors" title="Stoors">Stoors</a> come over the <a href="/wiki/Caradhras#The_Redhorn_Gate" title="Caradhras">Redhorn Pass</a> and move either to the <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Angle_of_Eriador" title="Angle of Eriador">Angle</a> or to <a href="/wiki/Dunland" title="Dunland">Dunland</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1191" title="Third Age 1191">1191</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Celepharn" title="Celepharn">Celepharn</a>. <a href="/wiki/Celebrindor" title="Celebrindor">Celebrindor</a> becomes the fifth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1194" title="Third Age 1194">1194</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Valacar" title="Valacar">Valacar</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1226" title="Third Age 1226">1226</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Atanatar_II" title="Atanatar II">Atanatar II Alcarin</a>. <a href="/wiki/Narmacil_I" title="Narmacil I">Narmacil I</a> becomes the seventeenth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup><br/>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Argeleb_I" title="Argeleb I">Argeleb I</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1240" title="Third Age 1240">1240</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/R%C3%B3mendacil_II" title="Rómendacil II">Minalcar</a> (later <i>Rómendacil II</i>) made <a href="/wiki/Regent_of_Gondor" title="Regent of Gondor">Regent of Gondor</a> by his uncle <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Narmacil_II" title="Narmacil II">Narmacil II</a>.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1248" title="Third Age 1248">1248</a></td>
<td><a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Minalcar" title="Minalcar">Minalcar</a> defeats a large army of <a href="/wiki/Easterlings" title="Easterlings">Easterlings</a>. He takes the name <i><a href="/wiki/R%C3%B3mendacil_II" title="Rómendacil II">Rómendacil</a></i>.<br/>About this time, construction on the <a href="/wiki/Argonath" title="Argonath">Argonath</a> begins.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1250" title="Third Age 1250">1250</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/R%C3%B3mendacil_II" title="Rómendacil II">Minalcar</a> (<i>Rómendacil II</i>) sends his son, <a href="/wiki/Valacar" title="Valacar">Valacar</a>, as an ambassador to the self-styled <a href="/wiki/King_of_Rhovanion" title="King of Rhovanion">King</a> of  <a href="/wiki/Rhovanion" title="Rhovanion">Rhovanion</a>, <a href="/wiki/Vidugavia" title="Vidugavia">Vidugavia</a>, prince of the <a href="/wiki/Northmen" title="Northmen">Northmen</a>, in order to better understand their customs.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1255" title="Third Age 1255">1255</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Eldacar_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Eldacar (King of Gondor)">Vinitharya</a> (<i>Eldacar</i>) of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a> in  <a href="/wiki/Rhovanion" title="Rhovanion">Rhovanion</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-16" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1259" title="Third Age 1259">1259</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Castamir" title="Castamir">Castamir</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1260" title="Third Age 1260">1260</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Valacar" title="Valacar">Valacar</a> recalled from  <a href="/wiki/Rhovanion" title="Rhovanion">Rhovanion</a>, by <a href="/wiki/R%C3%B3mendacil_II" title="Rómendacil II">his father</a>, to return to <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1272" title="Third Age 1272">1272</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Celebrindor" title="Celebrindor">Celebrindor</a>. <a href="/wiki/Malvegil" title="Malvegil">Malvegil</a> becomes the sixth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1294" title="Third Age 1294">1294</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Narmacil_I" title="Narmacil I">Narmacil I</a>. <a href="/wiki/Calmacil_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Calmacil (King of Gondor)">Calmacil</a> becomes the eighteenth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1300" title="Third Age 1300">1300</a></td>
<td>The <a href="/wiki/Nazg%C3%BBl" title="Nazgûl">Nazgûl</a> reappear. The <a href="/wiki/Witch-king" title="Witch-king">Witch-king</a> founds the <a href="/wiki/Angmar" title="Angmar">Kingdom of Angmar</a>. Orcs increase in the <a href="/wiki/Misty_Mountains" title="Misty Mountains">Misty Mountains</a> and attack the Dwarves. The <a href="/wiki/Hobbits" title="Hobbits">Hobbits</a> move westward and some settle in <a href="/wiki/Bree" title="Bree">Bree</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-19" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1304" title="Third Age 1304">1304</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Calmacil_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Calmacil (King of Gondor)">Calmacil</a>. <a href="/wiki/R%C3%B3mendacil_II" title="Rómendacil II">Rómendacil II</a> becomes the nineteenth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1309" title="Third Age 1309">1309</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Arveleg_I" title="Arveleg I">Arveleg I</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1330" title="Third Age 1330">1330</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Aldamir" title="Aldamir">Aldamir</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1332" title="Third Age 1332">1332</a></td>
<td>Possible death of <a href="/wiki/Vidumavi" title="Vidumavi">Vidumavi</a> (<i>Galadwen</i>), wife of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Valacar" title="Valacar">Valacar</a> and mother of <a href="/wiki/Eldacar_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Eldacar (King of Gondor)">Eldacar</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Vidumavi_8-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Vidumavi-8">[note 1]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1344" title="Third Age 1344">1344</a></td>
<td>Possible death of <a href="/wiki/Vidumavi" title="Vidumavi">Vidumavi</a> (<i>Galadwen</i>), wife of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Valacar" title="Valacar">Valacar</a> and mother of <a href="/wiki/Eldacar_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Eldacar (King of Gondor)">Eldacar</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Vidumavi_8-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Vidumavi-8">[note 1]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1349" title="Third Age 1349">1349</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Malvegil" title="Malvegil">Malvegil</a>. <a href="/wiki/Argeleb_I" title="Argeleb I">Argeleb I</a> becomes the seventh <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1356" title="Third Age 1356">1356</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Argeleb_I" title="Argeleb I">Argeleb I</a>. <a href="/wiki/Arveleg_I" title="Arveleg I">Arveleg I</a> becomes the eighth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup> About this time, some <a href="/wiki/Stoors" title="Stoors">Stoors</a> return to the <a href="/wiki/Gladden_Fields" title="Gladden Fields">Gladden Fields</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-20" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1366" title="Third Age 1366">1366</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/R%C3%B3mendacil_II" title="Rómendacil II">Rómendacil II</a>. <a href="/wiki/Valacar" title="Valacar">Valacar</a> becomes the twentieth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-16" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1391" title="Third Age 1391">1391</a></td>
<td>Births of <a href="/wiki/Araphor" title="Araphor">Araphor</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a> and <a href="/wiki/Hyarmendacil_II" title="Hyarmendacil II">Vinyarion</a> (<i>Hyarmendacil II</i>) of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-19" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1409" title="Third Age 1409">1409</a></td>
<td>The <a href="/wiki/Witch-king" title="Witch-king">Witch-king</a> of <a href="/wiki/Angmar" title="Angmar">Angmar</a> invades <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>, destroys the <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Tower_of_Amon_S%C3%BBl" title="Tower of Amon Sûl">Tower</a> of <a href="/wiki/Weathertop" title="Weathertop">Amon Sûl</a>, occupies <a href="/wiki/Rhudaur" title="Rhudaur">Rhudaur</a> and ravages <a href="/wiki/Cardolan" title="Cardolan">Cardolan</a>, slaying its <a href="/wiki/Last_prince_of_Cardolan" title="Last prince of Cardolan">last prince</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-9">[8]</a></sup> Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Arveleg_I" title="Arveleg I">Arveleg I</a>. <a href="/wiki/Araphor" title="Araphor">Araphor</a> becomes the ninth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1432" title="Third Age 1432">1432</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Valacar" title="Valacar">Valacar</a>. <a href="/wiki/Eldacar_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Eldacar (King of Gondor)">Eldacar</a> (born <i>Vinitharya</i>) becomes the twenty-first <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>. <b>Start of the <a href="/wiki/Kin-strife" title="Kin-strife">Kin-strife</a>.</b><sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-South_5-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1437" title="Third Age 1437">1437</a></td>
<td><a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Eldacar_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Eldacar (King of Gondor)">Eldacar</a> deposed. <a href="/wiki/Castamir" title="Castamir">Castamir &#34;the Usurper&#34;</a> becomes the twenty-second <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>. The <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Osgiliath-stone" title="Osgiliath-stone">Osgiliath-stone</a> is lost in the <a href="/wiki/Anduin" title="Anduin">Anduin</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-22" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-South_5-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1447" title="Third Age 1447">1447</a></td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Battle_of_the_Crossings_of_Erui" title="Battle of the Crossings of Erui">Battle of the Crossings of Erui</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Siege_of_Pelargir" title="Siege of Pelargir">Siege of Pelargir</a>. Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Castamir" title="Castamir">Castamir</a>. <a href="/wiki/Eldacar_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Eldacar (King of Gondor)">Eldacar</a> regains the kingship of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a> with the help of the Northmen and <a href="/wiki/D%C3%BAnedain" title="Dúnedain">Dúnedain</a> of Gondor. <b>End of the <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kin-Strife" title="Kin-Strife">Kin-Strife</a>.</b><sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-23" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1448" title="Third Age 1448">1448</a></td>
<td>Rebels of the sons of <a href="/wiki/Castamir" title="Castamir">Castamir</a> escape the <a href="/wiki/Siege_of_Pelargir" title="Siege of Pelargir">Siege of Pelargir</a> and seize the Haven of <a href="/wiki/Umbar" title="Umbar">Umbar</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-24" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1454" title="Third Age 1454">1454</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Minardil" title="Minardil">Minardil</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-20" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1473" title="Third Age 1473">1473</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Argeleb_II" title="Argeleb II">Argeleb II</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-16" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1490" title="Third Age 1490">1490</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Eldacar_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Eldacar (King of Gondor)">Eldacar</a>. <a href="/wiki/Aldamir" title="Aldamir">Aldamir</a> becomes the twenty-third <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-19" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1516" title="Third Age 1516">1516</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Telemnar" title="Telemnar">Telemnar</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1540" title="Third Age 1540">1540</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Aldamir" title="Aldamir">Aldamir</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-25" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Hyarmendacil_II" title="Hyarmendacil II">Vinyarion</a> becomes the twenty-fourth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-20" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1551" title="Third Age 1551">1551</a></td>
<td><a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Hyarmendacil_II" title="Hyarmendacil II">Vinyarion</a> defeats the <a href="/wiki/Haradrim" title="Haradrim">Haradrim</a> and assumes name <i>Hyarmendacil II</i>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-26" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1553" title="Third Age 1553">1553</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Arvegil" title="Arvegil">Arvegil</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arnor" title="Arnor">Arnor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1577" title="Third Age 1577">1577</a></td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Tarondor_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Tarondor (King of Gondor)">Tarondor</a> of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-22" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1589" title="Third Age 1589">1589</a></td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Araphor" title="Araphor">Araphor</a>. <a href="/wiki/Argeleb_II" title="Argeleb II">Argeleb II</a> becomes the tenth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Arthedain" title="Kings of Arthedain">King</a> of <a href="/wiki/Arthedain" title="Arthedain">Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-16" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr></tbody></table>

<table class="wikitable">
<!-- -->
<tbody><tr>
<th><b><a href="/wiki/Third_Age" title="Third Age">T.A.</a></b></th>
<th><b><a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Shire_Reckoning" title="Shire Reckoning">S.R.</a></b></th>
<th><b>Event</b>
</th></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1601" title="Third Age 1601">1601</a></td>
<td>1</td>
<td><b>Creation of <a href="/wiki/The_Shire" title="The Shire">the Shire</a>.</b> The count of years in <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Shire_Reckoning" title="Shire Reckoning">Shire Reckoning</a> begins with year 1.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1621" title="Third Age 1621">1621</a></td>
<td>21</td>
<td>Death of <a href="/wiki/Hyarmendacil_II" title="Hyarmendacil II">Hyarmendacil II</a>. <a href="/wiki/Minardil" title="Minardil">Minardil</a> becomes the twenty-fifth <a href="/wiki/King_of_Gondor" title="King of Gondor">King of Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1630" title="Third Age 1630">1630</a></td>
<td>30</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Stoors" title="Stoors">Stoors</a> from <a href="/wiki/Dunland" title="Dunland">Dunland</a> settle in <a href="/wiki/The_Shire" title="The Shire">the Shire</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-27" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1632" title="Third Age 1632">1632</a></td>
<td>32</td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Telumehtar_Umbardacil" title="Telumehtar Umbardacil">Telumehtar</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-23" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1633" title="Third Age 1633">1633</a></td>
<td>33</td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Arveleg_II" title="Arveleg II">Arveleg II</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Northern_4-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Northern-4">[4]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1634" title="Third Age 1634">1634</a></td>
<td>34</td>
<td>Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Minardil" title="Minardil">Minardil</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-28" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Telemnar" title="Telemnar">Telemnar</a> becomes the twenty-sixth <a href="/wiki/King_of_Gondor" title="King of Gondor">King of Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-22" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1635" title="Third Age 1635">1635</a></td>
<td>35</td>
<td>The <a href="/wiki/Great_Plague" title="Great Plague">Great Plague</a> hits <a href="/wiki/Rhovanion" title="Rhovanion">Rhovanion</a>.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1636" title="Third Age 1636">1636</a></td>
<td>36</td>
<td>The <a href="/wiki/Great_Plague" title="Great Plague">Great Plague</a> spreads to <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>. Death of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Telemnar" title="Telemnar">Telemnar</a> and all his children.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-29" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> The <a href="/wiki/White_Tree_of_Gondor#Second_White_Tree" title="White Tree of Gondor">White Tree</a> withers and dies. <a href="/wiki/Osgiliath" title="Osgiliath">Osgiliath</a> is left in ruins. <a href="/wiki/Barrow-wights" title="Barrow-wights">Barrow-wights</a> inhabit <a href="/wiki/Barrow-downs" title="Barrow-downs">Tyrn Gorthad</a>. <a href="/wiki/Tarondor_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Tarondor (King of Gondor)">Tarondor</a> becomes the twenty-seventh <a href="/wiki/King_of_Gondor" title="King of Gondor">King of Gondor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-South_5-23" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-South-5">[5]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1637" title="Third Age 1637">1637</a></td>
<td>37</td>
<td>The <a href="/wiki/Great_Plague" title="Great Plague">Great Plague</a> hits <a href="/wiki/The_Shire" title="The Shire">the Shire</a>. The <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Periannath" title="Periannath">Periannath</a> survive, but suffer great loss.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1640" title="Third Age 1640">1640</a></td>
<td>40</td>
<td>The capital of Gondor is offically moved to <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Minas_Anor" title="Minas Anor">Minas Anor</a>. <a href="/wiki/Mordor" title="Mordor">Mordor</a> is left unguarded. <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Kings_of_Gondor" title="Kings of Gondor">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Tarondor_(King_of_Gondor)" title="Tarondor (King of Gondor)">Tarondor</a> plants the <a href="/wiki/White_Tree_of_Gondor#Third_White_Tree" title="White Tree of Gondor">Seedling of the White Tree</a> in <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Minas_Anor" title="Minas Anor">Minas Anor</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-30" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1670" title="Third Age 1670">1670</a></td>
<td>70</td>
<td>Death of <a href="/wiki/Argeleb_II" title="Argeleb II">Argeleb II</a>. <a href="/wiki/Arvegil" title="Arvegil">Arvegil</a> becomes <a href="/wiki/King_of_Arthedain" title="King of Arthedain">King of Arthedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-North_6-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1684" title="Third Age 1684">1684</a></td>
<td>84</td>

Eärnil sends a fleet north carrying his son <a href="/wiki/E%C3%A4rnur" title="Eärnur">Eärnur</a> with an army.

<td>376</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Aranarth" title="Aranarth">Aranarth</a> becomes the first <a href="/wiki/Chieftain_of_the_D%C3%BAnedain" title="Chieftain of the Dúnedain">Chieftain of the Dúnedain</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-39" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-North_6-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-North-6">[6]</a></sup>

</td></tr>
<tr>

<td>380</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Nazg%C3%BBl" title="Nazgûl">Nazgûl</a> gather in <a href="/wiki/Mordor" title="Mordor">Mordor</a>. <b><a href="/wiki/Durin%27s_Bane" title="Durin&#39;s Bane">Durin&#39;s Bane</a> awakes in <a href="/wiki/Moria" title="Moria">Khazad-dûm</a>. Death of <a href="/wiki/Durin_VI" title="Durin VI">Durin VI</a>.</b><sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-41" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/N%C3%A1in_I" title="Náin I">Náin I</a> becomes <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/King_of_Durin%27s_folk" title="King of Durin&#39;s folk">King of Durin&#39;s folk</a>.

The <a href="/wiki/Silvan_Elves" title="Silvan Elves">Silvan Elves</a> flee south.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-42" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Galadriel" title="Galadriel">Galadriel</a> and <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Celeborn_(Lord_of_L%C3%B3rien)" title="Celeborn (Lord of Lórien)">Celeborn</a> return to <a href="/wiki/Lothl%C3%B3rien" title="Lothlórien">Laurelindórenan</a>.

<td>399</td>
<td>Birth of <a href="/wiki/Eradan" title="Eradan">Eradan</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Southern_3-33" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Southern-3">[3]</a></sup> <b><a href="/wiki/Lonely_Mountain" title="Lonely Mountain">Erebor</a> founded.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-43" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> The <a href="/wiki/Arkenstone" title="Arkenstone">Arkenstone</a> discovered.</b>

<td>400</td>
<td><b>The <a href="/wiki/Nazg%C3%BBl" title="Nazgûl">Nazgûl</a> issue from <a href="/wiki/Mordor" title="Mordor">Mordor</a> and besiege <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Minas_Ithil" title="Minas Ithil">Minas Ithil</a>.</b><sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-44" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>

</td></tr>
<tr>

<td><b>The <a href="/wiki/War_of_the_Dwarves_and_Orcs" title="War of the Dwarves and Orcs">War of the Dwarves and Orcs</a> begins.</b><sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-71" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>

The sixteenth Prince&#39;s unnamed son becomes the seventeenth <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Princes_of_Dol_Amroth" title="Princes of Dol Amroth">Prince</a> of <a href="/wiki/Dol_Amroth" title="Dol Amroth">Dol Amroth</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Dol_Amroth_11-34" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Dol_Amroth-11">[10]</a></sup>

<td>1351</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Sauron" title="Sauron">Sauron</a> sends three <a href="/wiki/Nazg%C3%BBl" title="Nazgûl">Nazgûl</a> to reoccupy <a href="/wiki/Dol_Guldur" title="Dol Guldur">Dol Guldur</a>. Construction begins on <a href="/wiki/Barad-d%C3%BBr" title="Barad-dûr">Barad-dûr</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-97" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> Birth of <a href="/wiki/Heribald_Bolger" title="Heribald Bolger">Heribald Bolger</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Bolger_14-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Bolger-14">[13]</a></sup>

He gives <a href="/wiki/Aragorn" title="Aragorn">Aragorn</a> the <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Shards_of_Narsil" title="Shards of Narsil">Shards of Narsil</a>. In the woods of <a href="/wiki/Rivendell" title="Rivendell">Rivendell</a>, <a href="/wiki/Aragorn" title="Aragorn">Aragorn</a> meets <a href="/wiki/Arwen" title="Arwen">Arwen</a>. <a href="/wiki/Aragorn" title="Aragorn">Aragorn</a> goes into the Wild.
</td></tr>

Deaths of <a href="/wiki/King_of_Rohan" title="King of Rohan">King</a> <a href="/wiki/Thengel" title="Thengel">Thengel</a>, <a href="/wiki/Fortinbras_Took_II" title="Fortinbras Took II">Fortinbras Took II</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-Took_13-43" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Took-13">[12]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Drogo_Baggins" title="Drogo Baggins">Drogo Baggins</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-Baggins_17-30" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Baggins-17">[16]</a></sup> and <a href="/wiki/Primula_Brandybuck" title="Primula Brandybuck">Primula Brandybuck</a> (<a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Baggins_Family" title="Baggins Family">Baggins</a>). <b><a href="/wiki/Th%C3%A9oden" title="Théoden">Théoden</a> becomes the seventeenth <a href="/wiki/King_of_Rohan" title="King of Rohan">King of Rohan</a>.</b><sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-106" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup> Births of <a href="/wiki/Fredegar_Bolger" title="Fredegar Gamgee</a></b>,<sup id="cite_ref-Gamgee_16-25" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Gamgee-16">[15]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Everard_Took" title="Everard Took">Everard Took</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-Took_13-44" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Took-13">[12]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Berilac_Brandybuck" title="Berilac Brandybuck">Berilac Brandybuck</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-Brandybuck_15-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Brandybuck-15">[14]</a></sup> and <a href="/wiki/Tolman_Cotton_Junior" title="Tolman Cotton Junior">Tolman Cotton Junior</a>.<sup href="/wiki/Thain" title="Thain">Thain</a> of <a href="/wiki/The_Shire" title="The Shire">the Shire</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Took_13-45" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Took-13">[12]</a></sup>

</td></tr>

</td></tr>

</td></tr>

</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_3017" title="Third Age 3017">3017</a></td>
<td>1417</td>
<td><b><a href="/wiki/Gollum" title="Gollum">Gollum</a> captured and taken to <a href="/wiki/Barad-d%C3%BBr" title="Barad-dûr">Barad-dûr</a>.</b> Birth of <a href="/wiki/Alphros" title="Alphros">Alphros</a>. <a href="/wiki/%C3%89omer" title="Éomer">Éomer</a> becomes <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Marshal" title="Marshal">Marshal</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Mark_12-31" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Mark-12">[11]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Gandalf" title="Gandalf">Gandalf</a> reads the scroll of <a href="/wiki/Isildur" title="Isildur">Isildur</a> in <a href="/wiki/Minas_Tirith" title="Minas Tirith">Minas Tirith</a>. <sup id="cite_ref-TA_2-121" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-TA-2">[2]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_3018" title="Third Age 3018">3018</a></td>
<td>1418</td>
<td><b>The <a href="/wiki/War_of_the_Ring" title="War of the Ring">War of the Ring</a> begins.</b> <a href="/wiki/Thranduil" title="Thranduil">Thranduil</a>&#39;s kingdom in <a href="/wiki/Mirkwood" title="Mirkwood">Mirkwood</a> assailed by <a href="/wiki/Orcs" title="Orcs">Orcs</a>. <a href="/wiki/Osgiliath" title="Osgiliath">Osgiliath</a>, and much land east of the <a href="/wiki/Anduin" title="Anduin">Anduin</a>, is taken under control of <a href="/wiki/Mordor" title="Mordor">Mordor</a>. <a href="/wiki/Gollum" title="Gollum">Gollum</a> hides in <a href="/wiki/Moria" title="Moria">Moria</a>. <a href="/wiki/Gandalf" title="Gandalf">Gandalf</a> learns of the treachery of <a href="/wiki/Saruman" title="Saruman">Saruman</a>. <b><a href="/wiki/Frodo_Baggins" title="Frodo Baggins">Frodo</a> reaches <a href="/wiki/Rivendell" title="Rivendell">Rivendell</a>. The <a href="/wiki/Council_of_Elrond" title="Council of Elrond">Council of Elrond</a> is held and the <a href="/wiki/Fellowship_of_the_Ring" title="Fellowship of the Ring">Fellowship of the Ring</a> formed.</b>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_3019" title="Third Age 3019">3019</a></td>
<td>1419</td>
<td><b>The <a href="/wiki/Quest_of_the_Ring" title="Quest of the Ring">Quest of the Ring</a>.</b> <a href="/wiki/Gandalf" title="Gandalf">Gandalf</a> falls at the hand of a <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Balrog" title="Balrog">Balrog</a> in the Mines of <a href="/wiki/Moria" title="Moria">Moria</a>. <a href="/wiki/Boromir" title="Boromir">Boromir</a> dies defending <a href="/wiki/Meriadoc_Brandybuck" title="Meriadoc Brandybuck">Merry</a> and <a href="/wiki/Peregrin_Took" title="Peregrin Took">Pippin</a> from <a href="/wiki/Saruman" title="Saruman">Saruman</a>&#39;s <a href="/wiki/Uruk-hai" title="Uruk-hai">Uruk-hai</a>. The <a href="/wiki/Breaking_of_the_Fellowship" title="Breaking of the Fellowship">Breaking of the Fellowship</a>. <a href="/wiki/Gollum" title="Gollum">Gollum</a> leads <a href="/wiki/Frodo_Baggins" title="Frodo Baggins">Frodo</a> and <a href="/wiki/Samwise_Gamgee" title="Samwise Gamgee">Sam</a> into <a href="/wiki/Mordor" title="Mordor">Mordor</a>. <a href="/wiki/Gandalf" title="Gandalf">Gandalf</a> returns as Gandalf the White and frees <a href="/wiki/Th%C3%A9oden" title="Théoden">Théoden</a> from the hold of <a href="/wiki/Saruman" title="Saruman">Saruman</a>. <b>The <a href="/wiki/Battle_of_the_Hornburg" title="Battle of the Hornburg">Battle of Helm&#39;s Deep</a>.</b> The <a href="/wiki/Ents" title="Ents">Ents</a> fight the <b><a href="/wiki/Battle_of_Isengard" title="Battle of Isengard">Battle of Isengard</a></b>. <a href="/wiki/Aragorn" title="Aragorn">Aragorn</a> walks the <a href="/wiki/Paths_of_the_Dead" title="Paths of the Dead">Paths of the Dead</a>. Simultaneous attacks on <a href="/wiki/Thranduil" title="Thranduil">Thranduil</a>, <a href="/wiki/Lothl%C3%B3rien" title="Lothlórien">Lothlórien</a> and <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>. <b>The <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Battle_of_Pelennor_Fields" title="Battle of Pelennor Fields">Battle of Pelennor Fields</a></b>. Deaths of <a href="/wiki/Denethor" title="Denethor">Denethor II</a>, <a href="/wiki/Th%C3%A9oden" title="Théoden">Théoden</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Witch-king" title="Witch-king">Witch-king</a> of Angmar. Assault upon <a href="/wiki/Dale" title="Dale">Dale</a> and <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Erebor" title="Erebor">Erebor</a>. Deaths of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/D%C3%A1in_II_Ironfoot" title="Dáin II Ironfoot">Dáin II</a> and <a href="/wiki/Brand" title="Brand">Brand</a>. <b>The <a href="/wiki/Battle_of_the_Morannon" title="Battle of the Morannon">Battle of the Morannon</a>. <a href="/wiki/The_One_Ring" title="The One Ring">The One Ring</a> destroyed. End of <a href="/wiki/Sauron" title="Sauron">Sauron</a>.</b> <a href="/wiki/Nazg%C3%BBl" title="Nazgûl">Nazgûl</a> destroyed. Death of <a href="/wiki/Gollum" title="Gollum">Gollum</a>. <a href="/wiki/Bard_II" title="Bard II">Bard II</a> becomes <a href="/wiki/King_of_Dale" title="King of Dale">King of Dale</a>. <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Thorin_III_Stonehelm" title="Thorin III Stonehelm">Thorin III Stonehelm</a> becomes <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/King_of_Erebor" title="King of Erebor">King of Erebor</a>. <a href="/wiki/%C3%89omer" title="Éomer">Éomer</a> becomes the eighteenth <a href="/wiki/King_of_Rohan" title="King of Rohan">King of Rohan</a>. <a href="/wiki/Aragorn" title="Aragorn">Aragorn</a> takes the name <i>Elessar</i> and is crowned the thirty-fourth <a href="/wiki/King_of_Gondor" title="King of Gondor">King of Gondor</a>. He receives the [[Sceptre of Annúmina], becoming the first High King of the <a href="/wiki/Reunited_Kingdom" title="Reunited Kingdom">Reunited Kingdom</a>, and marries <a href="/wiki/Arwen" title="Arwen">Arwen</a>. <a href="/wiki/Mirkwood" title="Mirkwood">Mirkwood</a> renamed <a href="/wiki/Eryn_Lasgalen" title="Eryn Lasgalen">Eryn Lasgalen</a>. <a href="/wiki/East_L%C3%B3rien" title="East Lórien">East Lórien</a> founded. <b>The <a href="/wiki/Battle_of_Bywater" title="Battle of Bywater">Scouring of the Shire</a>.</b> Deaths of <a href="/wiki/Lotho_Sackville-Baggins" title="Lotho Sackville-Baggins">Lotho Sackville-Baggins</a>, <a href="/wiki/Saruman" title="Saruman">Saruman</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Gr%C3%ADma" title="Gríma">Gríma</a> Wormtongue. <b>End of the <a href="/wiki/War_of_the_Ring" title="War of the Ring">War of the Ring</a></b>.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_3020" title="Third Age 3020">3020</a></td>
<td>1420</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Dr%C3%BAedain" title="Drúedain">Drúedain</a> destroy remnant of <a href="/wiki/Saruman" title="Saruman">Saruman</a>&#39;s <a href="/wiki/Orcs" title="Orcs">Orcs</a>. <a href="/wiki/Faramir" title="Faramir">Faramir</a> and <a href="/wiki/%C3%89owyn" title="Éowyn">Éowyn</a> wed.<sup id="cite_ref-22" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-22">[20]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Samwise_Gamgee" title="Samwise Gamgee">Samwise</a> weds <a href="/wiki/Rose_Cotton" title="Rose Cotton">Rose Cotton</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-23" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-23">[21]</a></sup> Death of <a href="/wiki/Lobelia_Sackville-Baggins" title="Lobelia Sackville-Baggins">Lobelia Bracegirdle</a> (<a href="/wiki/Sackville-Baggins_Family" title="Sackville-Baggins Family">Sackville-Baggins</a>).<sup id="cite_ref-24" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-24">[22]</a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/Third_Age_3021" title="Third Age 3021">3021</a></td>
<td>1421</td>
<td>Birth of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Elanor_Gamgee" title="Elanor Gamgee">Elanor Gamgee</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Gamgee_16-31" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Gamgee-16">[15]</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/%C3%89omer" title="Éomer">Éomer</a> and <a href="/wiki/Loth%C3%ADriel" title="Lothíriel">Lothíriel</a> wed.<sup id="cite_ref-25" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-25">[23]</a></sup> <b><a href="/wiki/Elrond" title="Elrond">Elrond</a>, <a href="/wiki/Galadriel" title="Galadriel">Galadriel</a>, <a href="/wiki/Gandalf" title="Gandalf">Gandalf</a>, <a href="/wiki/Bilbo_Baggins" title="Bilbo Baggins">Bilbo</a> and <a href="/wiki/Frodo_Baggins" title="Frodo Baggins">Frodo</a> pass over the <a href="/wiki/Belegaer" title="Belegaer">Sea</a>.</b><sup id="cite_ref-26" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-26">[24]</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-27" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-27">[25]</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-28" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-28">[note 3]</a></sup>
</td></tr></tbody></table>

## Notes {#Notes}

<span><div class="mw-references-wrap"><ol class="references">
<li id="cite_note-Vidumavi-8"><span class="mw-cite-backlink">↑ <sup><a href="#cite_ref-Vidumavi_8-0">1.0</a></sup> <sup><a href="#cite_ref-Vidumavi_8-1">1.1</a></sup></span> <span class="reference-text">Tolkien has conflicting dates for as Vidumavi&#39;s year of death; two copies of the same typescript provide different dates (<a href="/wiki/Third_Age" title="Third Age">T.A.</a> <a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1332" title="Third Age 1332">1332</a> or <a href="/wiki/Third_Age" title="Third Age">T.A.</a> <a href="/wiki/Third_Age_1344" title="Third Age 1344">1344</a>).</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-21"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><a href="#cite_ref-21">↑</a></span> <span class="reference-text">In earlier editions, Appendix C gives his death as S.R. 1360, but in some of the later editions the year is mistakenly given as 1363.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-28"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><a href="#cite_ref-28">↑</a></span> <span class="reference-text">In the reckoning of <a href="/wiki/Gondor" title="Gondor">Gondor</a>, for record-keeping purposes, the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_Age" title="Fourth Age">Fourth Age</a> began on <a href="/wiki/25_March" title="25 March">25 March</a> of <a href="/wiki/Third_Age" title="Third Age">T.A.</a> <a href="/wiki/Third_Age_3021" title="Third Age 3021">3021</a>. The Third Age was held to have actually ended when the <a href="/wiki/Three_Keepers" title="Three Keepers">Three Keepers</a> departed over the Sea on <a href="/wiki/29_September" title="29 September">29 September</a> of 3021. The <a href="/wiki/Hobbits" title="Hobbits">Hobbits</a> held that the Fourth Age began on 2 Yule of <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Shire_Reckoning" title="Shire Reckoning">S.R.</a> <a href="/wiki/Fourth_Age_1" title="Fourth Age 1">1422</a>, which would have been in <a href="/wiki/Third_Age" title="Third Age">T.A.</a> <a class="mw-redirect" href="/wiki/Third_Age_3022" title="Third Age 3022">3022</a>, had the count of Third Age years continued.</span>
</li>
</ol></div></span>
